		  	$('.menu-search').css({
		  		'position':'fixed',
		  	 	'z-index':'1030',
		  	  	'top':'0'});
	  	}
	  	if ($(this).scrollTop() < 120) {
		   	$('.menu-search').removeClass('navbar-fixed-top menu-search-scroll');
		   	$('.menu-search-inner').removeClass('menu-search-inner-scroll');
		   	$('.nav-inner').removeClass('nav-inner-scroll');
		   	$('.menu-search').css({
		   		'position':'static', 
		   		'z-index':'1030', 
		   		'top':'auto'});
	  	}
	});
});</script> </div></div></div></div> </header></div><div class="banner7"><div class="container"><div class="row"><div class="col-md-9 col-sm-9 col-sms-12"><div class="pos-slideshow"><div class="flexslider ma-nivoslider"><div class="pos-loading" style="display: none;"></div><div class="slides nivoSlider" id="pos-slideshow-home"> <img alt="" data-thumb="https://thearcheryshop.co.uk/img/blockslideshow/141.jpg" src="https://thearcheryshop.co.uk/img/blockslideshow/141.jpg" style="display: none;" title="#htmlcaption141"/> <img alt="" data-thumb="https://thearcheryshop.co.uk/img/blockslideshow/139.jpg" src="https://thearcheryshop.co.uk/img/blockslideshow/139.jpg" style="display: none;" title="#htmlcaption139"/><img class="nivo-main-image" src="https://thearcheryshop.co.uk/img/blockslideshow/141.jpg" style="display: inline;"/><div class="nivo-caption"><div class="timing-bar" style=" position:absolute;
